## D-043 — ORDER-EDIT-001: a sent-order edit is an in-place delta edit of the SAME open, unpaid order — `order.edit` → `app.edit_order`, append-only money-free `order_edits`, no new states

- **Status:** **Owner-approved** by the human owner, Saleh, on 2026-10-08 under Work ID **ORDER-EDIT-001**: owner decisions 1 and 2 and the defaults he confirmed the same day (tax, discount, finished food on printer-only branches, audit coverage in ORDER-EDIT-001A), recorded in the merged design note [ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN](ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN.md) §4 (PR #302), which stays the design record. Processed under the architecture-change procedure ([AGENT_WORKFLOW.md](AGENT_WORKFLOW.md) §9): a dedicated Work ID, this DECISIONS entry, independent read-only review, and human approval at the merge gate. **Takes effect when the ORDER-EDIT-001 register transcription is merged to `main`.** On the owner's explicit instruction (design header rule (c), 2026-10-08) it is written on `main` without waiting for open PR #288; its IDs were re-verified free on `main` and on #288 head `9d817a34` (rule (b)). The transcription is documentation only: no code, migration, test, l10n or CI change accompanies it. Implementation follows in the design §10 slices, each under its own Work ID, branch and PR, with its own tests, independent read-only review and owner approval before merge — the Work IDs proposed in design §10, pending owner approval: KITCHEN-DISPATCH-HARDEN-001, POS-ORDER-DETAIL-IDS-001, ORDER-EDIT-001A, ORDER-EDIT-001B, ORDER-EDIT-001C, ORDER-EDIT-001D, ORDER-EDIT-001E, ORDER-EDIT-001F, ORDER-EDIT-001G and ORDER-EDIT-001R (release and enablement, no code); none of them moves to Ready before this transcription is merged. Amends the frozen baseline in [STATE_MACHINES](STATE_MACHINES.md) §1, §1.1, §1.2, §2.1–§2.2 and §11 (and adds the descriptive service-round §1.3), [MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC](MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC.md) §4.4, §4.5, §6, §9 (new §9.2), §12.2, §13 and §19, [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md), [SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md), [DOMAIN_MODEL](DOMAIN_MODEL.md), [OFFLINE_SYNC_SPEC](OFFLINE_SYNC_SPEC.md) and [TESTING_STRATEGY](TESTING_STRATEGY.md) (see Related); the **D-018** enumerations are unchanged.
- **Context:** Customers change their minds after an order reaches the kitchen ("the burger without tomato", "remove the fries", "one more cola"), and the owner wants the cashier to edit any open, unpaid order normally, with prices recalculated and the kitchen told at once. After submit the code allows only three changes (verified at `86a3e247`, design §5): add items (`order.items_add` → `app.add_order_items`, PSC-001C service rounds), a discount (`app.apply_discount`, whose item-scope path rewrites one live line's `line_discount_minor`), and a whole-order void (`app.void_order`, with the PSC-001D KDS acknowledgement and the printer-only VOID dispatch). Nothing in SQL, the sync ops or the POS can void a single item or change its quantity or options; [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.2 `update_order_item` and §4.6 `void_item` are docs-only. The owner's first idea — silently cancel the order and send a new one carrying the **same order number** — cannot work here: the `#XXXXXX` code is the last six hex characters of the order's client-minted UUID (there is no order-number column, and `receipt_number` is assigned only at payment, **D-021**), so "same number" means "same row", and a new UUID with the same tail would counterfeit the code in history, audit and tickets; the old order cannot disappear (DELETE is revoked and denied by RLS — `orders_del_deny`, `order_items_del_deny`; child rows reference orders `ON DELETE RESTRICT`; the append-only audit already holds `order.submitted`, **D-013**; deletion is a tombstone or a state change only, **D-020**; voided amounts are never silently dropped, MONEY §13); the only legal retirement is a void, which is exactly the "cancelled order" the owner wants to avoid; a full re-send after cooking has started is either cooked twice or misses the removal, and printed paper cannot be recalled; and taking the cash and then rewriting the bill is the classic cashier fraud pattern that D-013, D-020 and MONEY §13 exist to close.
- **Decision:** **DECISION D-043** —
  1. **Delete-and-recreate is rejected.** No new order row, no reused or counterfeit `#code`, no silent cancellation. Kept from the owner's idea: the same order and number, normal editing, recalculated prices, the kitchen told at once (**D-044**) and never a "cancelled order". The one goal deliberately not met is "no record": every edit is recorded as an **order edit**, never as a cancellation.
  2. **The same row, one append-only record per edit.** An edit changes the same `orders` row, which keeps its UUID, code, table, shift and report bucket (`created_at`). Each applied edit writes one append-only, **money-free** `public.order_edits` row: org / restaurant / branch and order composite FKs (`RESTRICT`); `edit_number` ≥ 1, unique per order, allocated as `orders.edit_count` + 1 under the order lock; `device_id` + `local_operation_id` unique per organization (**D-022**); the PIN session, employee and membership; `reason_code` in (`customer_changed_mind`, `entry_mistake`, `item_unavailable`, `kitchen_issue`, `other`) or null, and `reason_text` ≤ 200; `kitchen_channel` in (`kds`, `paper`); `kitchen_ack_required`, with the acknowledgement triple (time, employee, device) set all-or-none, only when required, write-once; `bill_presented_at`; timestamps and `deleted_at`. FORCE RLS: SELECT scoped like `order_service_rounds`; INSERT / UPDATE / DELETE denied to clients; a trigger lets only the one-time acknowledgement stamp and `updated_at` change. New `orders.edit_count` (int ≥ 0, default 0); every edit increments `edit_count` and `orders.revision` by one. Fields are owned by [DOMAIN_MODEL](DOMAIN_MODEL.md) §6.1, §6.2 and §6.4.
  3. **Eligibility (v1), online only, behind a switch.** An order may be edited only when all hold: type `dine_in` or `takeaway`; status `submitted`, `accepted`, `preparing`, `ready` or `served`; **no live completed payment** (the `app.add_order_items` predicate); a POS device and a cashier, manager, restaurant_owner or org_owner actor; the device online; the order's submit acknowledged by the server (not still in the outbox, **OPEN QUESTION Q-045**); and the branch switch `branches.order_edit_enabled` ON. The edit travels only as the online-only direct sync op `order.edit` → `app.edit_order(p_pin_session_id, p_order_id, p_device_id, p_local_operation_id, p_payload jsonb, p_client_created_at default null) → jsonb` through `app.sync_push` (no outbox, no client grant), payload `{order_id, reason_code?, reason_text?, bill_presented_at?, expected: {subtotal_minor, tax_total_minor, grand_total_minor}, changes[1..100]}`. Refusals — each RETURNed (error / detail), audited `order.edit_denied`, and **decided before the first write** (`app.sync_push` commits a RETURNed envelope; only a RAISE rolls back): caller — the device-type and role refusals exactly as `app.add_order_items` returns them; shape `invalid_payload`, `no_changes`, `too_many_changes`, `duplicate_line_reference`, `expected_totals_required`; gates `feature_disabled`, `order_not_editable`, `order_already_settled`, `kitchen_mode_changed`; lines `line_changed` `{stale_ids}`, `line_has_discount`, `legacy_line_not_editable`; authority `permission_denied` / `removal_not_permitted`, `reason_required`, `permission_denied` / `finished_food_needs_manager`; new lines `item_unavailable`, `modifier_option_not_in_scope`, `modifier_prep_snapshot_stale`, `invalid_item_payload`; plan `edit_would_empty_order`; money `invalid_discount` / `discount_exceeds_order_total`, `tax_mode_unsupported`, `permission_denied` / `full_comp_permission_required` (the pairs `app.apply_discount` already returns, [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.5, so the existing POS and Activity-log mappings and l10n keys are reused), and `totals_mismatch` with the server's figures. RAISEs: **42501** on a structural failure of the PIN-session preamble and for an order outside the session's scope (anti-oracle), **40001** when the operation id was already used for an edit of another order. The two per-branch switches, `order_edit_enabled` and `order_edit_finished_food_manager_only` (both default false), are set only by the new `app.set_branch_order_edit_settings` (restaurant_owner or above; idempotent; audited `settings.branch.order_edit_updated`) behind a `SECURITY INVOKER` public wrapper that revokes PUBLIC and `anon` explicitly (**D-037**). Order-level fields (type, table, customer, order note) are not edited here; table moves keep the existing Move table action. Contract: [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.45.
  4. **Change kinds, applied as close-and-insert, with no new states.** Each `order_item_id` appears at most once: `remove`; `set_quantity` (1..999, ≠ current); `modify` (replacements[1..20], each with quantity, notes and modifiers — modifiers and note only: the size, variant and base price are copied from the old row server-side, and a size or variant change is a remove plus an add; "apply to all N / just 1" is expressed as replacements, an unchanged replacement being a continuation; a replacement carries no item-level `prep_snapshot`, the server builds it); `add` (the exact `order.items_add` item shape, `line_discount_minor` 0). The old line is retired and continuation, replacement or delta rows are inserted. **No state is added, renamed or repurposed (D-018):** a retired line becomes `cancelled` iff its work unit was still `submitted` on the KDS channel, otherwise `voided`, with `void_reason = 'order_edit:<reason>'`. New `order_items` columns `edit_id` and `removed_by_edit_id` (composite FKs to `order_edits`), `replaces_order_item_id` (composite FK to `order_items`, which gains UNIQUE (`organization_id`, `order_id`, `id`) as the FK target) and `removed_kitchen_stage` in (`submitted`, `accepted`, `preparing`, `ready`, `served`, `printed`) are **provenance, not states**. CHECKs: removed ⇒ status in (`voided`, `cancelled`); removed ⇔ stage recorded; replaces ⇒ `edit_id`; a write-once trigger guards the four columns. In-place rows keep the old `line_position` (a legacy old row with `line_position` 0 lands at max + 1 through the PRINT-LAYOUT-001D trigger, accepted).
  5. **Work units and landing.** The stage is judged per **work unit**, read under the lock at commit — the original ticket's stage is `orders.status`, a round's is its own status — never per dish, because item statuses do not advance. Landing (by unit stage on the KDS channel; the paper channel is one column of its own): **remove** → `cancelled` while the unit is Waiting (`submitted`, KDS), otherwise (In kitchen, Ready / Served, or paper) `voided`; **reduce** → the old line retired and the remainder written in place, at every stage and on paper (nothing is re-cooked); **increase** → a +N delta row in place while the unit is Waiting or In kitchen (`accepted` / `preparing`, KDS), otherwise (Ready / Served, or paper) in the edit's round; **modify** → the old line retired and the replacements in place while Waiting or In kitchen (KDS), while a Ready / Served unit (KDS) gets its changed replacements in the edit's round as REMAKE and the paper channel gets them in the edit's round as CHANGE; **add** → into the original ticket while it is Waiting (KDS), otherwise (In kitchen, Ready / Served, or paper) into the edit's round. On the paper channel every touched line counts as stage `printed`, and removed lines become `voided`. An edit opens **at most one** new round (`order_service_rounds.edit_id`, round number max + 1, status `submitted`); on paper that round gets no separate dispatch or print (the change slip covers it, **D-044**) and is closed at completion by `terminalize_printer_only_rounds`, as today.
  6. **Unit closure.** A round emptied by the edit while `submitted`..`ready` becomes `voided` with the new `order_service_rounds.voided_by_edit_id` (CHECK ⇒ status `voided`), and `app.order_rounds_all_served` ignores **only** rounds with `voided_by_edit_id` — superseding the PSC-001C "served only" lock for this case only. An emptied original unit in `submitted`..`ready`, while other live lines remain, moves the order to `served` **without stamping** `ready_at`: it stays NULL when the unit never reached `ready`, and an existing write-once stamp is preserved, never cleared or changed (PSC-001C; `orders_ready_at_check` allows it on `served`); the jump writes no ready-feed occurrence. This amends [STATE_MACHINES](STATE_MACHINES.md) §1.1 with server-only `submitted → served`, `accepted → served` and `preparing → served` rows (actor: server, only inside `app.edit_order`; audited through `order.edited`; online only; not reversible) and a note that `app.edit_order` may also apply the existing `ready → served` edge for an emptied original unit; and §1.2, whose `submitted → served` prohibition now names exactly **two** server-only routes, neither reachable by a client: `app.apply_direct_print_dispatch` (printer_only dispatch; still the only *dispatch* route to that edge) and `app.edit_order` (an emptied original unit on either kitchen mode, only while other live lines remain and only on an unpaid order). This `served` closes the emptied original ticket; it is **not** a customer pickup and **not** a table service — the order still has an active round holding the edit's new or REMAKE lines — and the jump stays because completion could otherwise never be reached. The §1 takeaway rule ("served is the customer pickup, displayed Picked up") is narrowed: while any service round of the order is active (`submitted`..`ready`), the POS and the Dashboard show the round's stage ("In kitchen" / "Ready"), never "Picked up" / "Served"; the KDS is unchanged. `app.try_auto_complete_order` runs under the held lock at the end of every edit.
  7. **Money — rules M1–M13, now owned by [MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC](MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC.md) §9.2, with §4.4, §4.5, §6, §12.2 and §13** (**D-007**, **D-008**; integer minor units throughout).
     - **M1** Kept lines are untouched: rows, modifiers, snapshots, item discount and legacy pricing epoch.
     - **M1a** A line L is **legacy-priced** iff `L.line_total_minor + L.line_discount_minor <> L.quantity × (L.unit_price_minor_snapshot + COALESCE(Σ m.price_minor_snapshot × m.quantity over L's order_item_modifiers, 0))`. The predicate is derived from the stored row only (the 002A per-unit formula change added no pricing-epoch column), computed **server-side only** by one internal helper shared by `app.edit_order` and `app.pos_order_detail` (its per-item `legacy` boolean); the POS only reads the flag. A row on which both epoch formulas agree (quantity 1, or no priced modifiers) is not legacy and is editable.
     - **M2** Retired lines keep their row and amounts for history; their status excludes them from every sum.
     - **M3** Continuation, replacement and delta rows copy **server-side** from the old row the base price, the size / variant and name snapshots, the display-order snapshots and every kept option's price and name snapshots. The display-order snapshots are restored by an UPDATE after the insert, because the MENU-ORDER-001 BEFORE INSERT triggers always re-derive them from the live menu; the triggers are not changed. The item `prep_snapshot` and each kept option's `meat_snapshot` are copied frozen, except that each `classifier_selected` is re-answered by the presence of its `classifier_option_id` in the replacement's full option set — the `app.trusted_modifier_prep_snapshot` / `app.trusted_item_prep_snapshot` presence rule, applied to the frozen link and never to the live menu. Only newly chosen options carry a client snapshot; it passes the 003D ownership check and the 021 prep-staleness check against `app.trusted_modifier_prep_snapshot(org, menu_item_id, option_id, <the replacement's full modifier array>)`, and new options keep the live-menu display ranks. `line_total = qty × (unit + Σ mod_price × mod_qty)` (the 002A per-unit formula), item discount 0. Lines with an item discount (`line_has_discount`) or a legacy price (`legacy_line_not_editable`) can only be removed.
     - **M4** Added lines follow `app.add_order_items` exactly (current menu price, its checks); sellable / available is checked under the lock for adds, quantity increases and a modify that raises the total quantity.
     - **M5** `subtotal_minor` is **re-rolled** as the sum of live line totals, never adjusted by a delta, so gross − item discount − order discount = net in reports.
     - **M6** `discount_total_minor` keeps its stored absolute amount; if it would exceed the new subtotal the edit is refused (`invalid_discount` / `discount_exceeds_order_total`) and the POS offers "Lower discount" — never clamped. The audit records the effective discount ratio before and after. Re-applying a percentage is **OPEN QUESTION Q-042**.
     - **M7** `tax_total_minor` is recomputed by the new `app.edit_tax_minor` from the branch's **current** tax settings on base = subtotal − discount: disabled or 0 bp → 0; `exclusive` → round-half-away(base × bp / 10000), byte-matching the POS `apps/pos/lib/src/format/tax_math.dart`; `inclusive` → `tax_mode_unsupported` (fail closed). No effect while tax is OFF (the branch default). The recompute also corrects earlier untaxed add-items lines on that order (`app.add_order_items` adds a subtotal delta and keeps the stored tax). Tax-rate snapshot and inclusive tax: **OPEN QUESTION Q-043**.
     - **M8** grand = subtotal − discount + tax ≥ 0. **Zero-out guard:** a total going from > 0 to 0 needs manager+ or the `apply_full_comp` capability, with or without a discount (`permission_denied` / `full_comp_permission_required`, the token `app.apply_discount` already emits); otherwise a cashier could leave a ₪0 line and let the order auto-complete unpaid.
     - **M9** `expected` totals are mandatory (`expected_totals_required`); any mismatch → `totals_mismatch` with the server's figures and nothing written (MONEY §1 rule 3).
     - **M10** A live completed payment → `order_already_settled`; paid orders need the deferred refund flow (**D-023**, **D-024**).
     - **M11** Every edit bumps `orders.revision`, so a payment prepared against the old total (payments send `expected_revision`) gets 40001 and re-reads the new total; the kitchen acknowledgement never bumps revision (**D-044**).
     - **M12** `receipt_number` (**D-021**), shift expected cash and the order's report bucket (`created_at`) are unaffected; `order_edits`, rounds and kitchen payloads carry no money (T-003).
     - **M13** Lines retired by an edit are excluded from Gross, Discounts, Net **and** the Voids bucket and are reported only in the new **Order edits** bucket (point 10), which amends MONEY §13 and the §12.2 "Money effect" line for edit-retired lines only.
  8. **Authority.** POS devices only; roles cashier, manager, restaurant_owner, org_owner; branch switch ON. Every `set_quantity` is classified server-side, on the locked line, as an **increase** (above the current quantity) or a **reduction** (below it). **Adding changes** — `add` and increases — need no extra right and no reason (parity with add items), so a cashier whose `void_order` is denied may still send an edit made only of adds and increases. **Removing changes** — `remove`, `modify` and reductions — by a cashier use the existing default-ON, deny-only `void_order` capability (`app.cashier_capability_allowed(..., 'void_order')`, owner decision 2), else `permission_denied` / `removal_not_permitted`; manager, restaurant_owner and org_owner are allowed by role (`app.edit_order` ORs the resolver's `void_order` answer with those role grants, as the existing enforcing RPCs do; the resolver is unchanged). The Staff screen label becomes "Cancel orders / remove sent items". This amends the [STATE_MACHINES](STATE_MACHINES.md) §2.1 actor column for **edit-originated** item `cancelled` / `voided` on **unpaid** orders only, and resolves its §2.1-versus-§2.2 contradiction for that case (§2.2's last Forbidden bullet is narrowed to match); a standalone item void stays manager+ as specified. **Finished food:** with `order_edit_finished_food_manager_only` ON (default OFF, owner decision 2), on the KDS channel only manager+ may remove, reduce or modify a line in a `ready` or `served` unit; a cashier gets `permission_denied` / `finished_food_needs_manager`. **Paper channel:** with no kitchen screen the system cannot tell whether food is cooked — direct-print orders and lines are stored `served` at submit, but every paper line counts as stage `printed` — so the switch has no effect there and any cashier with `void_order` may remove, reduce or change sent food (owner default, 2026-10-08); the Dashboard says so on printer-only branches, and the stored value is kept so it applies if the branch moves to KDS (**OPEN QUESTION Q-046**). **Reason:** any removing change requires `reason_code`, and `other` requires `reason_text`, else `reason_required`; the POS preselects "Customer changed mind" only when every such change touches a ticket the kitchen has not yet acknowledged (stage Waiting) — a convenience default only, still recorded and reported. Inline manager approval on the same till is **OPEN QUESTION Q-044**. **Online only:** the PIN session and membership are re-validated at apply time (**RISK R-007**). [SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §5 and [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.30 name `app.edit_order` (removing changes only) as an enforcing RPC of `void_order`; paid orders stay blocked.
  9. **Audit** (append-only, in the same transaction, **D-013**). `order.edited` — actor, device, scope and reason; old values (revision, status, edit count, totals, discount ratio); new values (code, edit number, change counts, kitchen outcome, totals, bill presented, role, device type, operation id, and `changes[≤100]` with per-line before / after quantities and totals, unit stage, landing and outcome status): the tamper-evident per-line record. `order.edit_denied` for every RETURNed refusal; `order.edit_acknowledged` / `order.edit_ack_denied` (**D-044**); `kitchen.dispatch_created` with `dispatch_type` `order_edit` (**D-044**); `settings.branch.order_edit_updated`. Under `app.audit_category` (current definition in `20260725090000_kitchen_mode_001c1_dispatch_ledger.sql`) the four `order.edit*` actions fall under "orders" via `order.%` — none starts with `order.void` or `order.discount`, so an edit never lands under voids or discounts — `kitchen.dispatch_created` under "orders" via the KITCHEN-MODE-001C1 `kitchen.%` rule, and `settings.branch.order_edit_updated` under "settings". The complete [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.33 coverage ships **in the same PR as each writer**, all in ORDER-EDIT-001A: `audit_safe_detail` / `audit_action_has_detail`, pgTAP writer and `audit_category` tests, the Dashboard `kAuditActionRegistry` entries with ar / he / en titles, the `_displayableKeys` labels and a green `auditRegistryViolations` guard. ORDER-EDIT-001A therefore also touches `packages/l10n` and `apps/dashboard`, an owner-approved (2026-10-08) exception to the shared-package split following the PSC-001D and POS-CASH-DRAWER-MANUAL-OPEN-001 precedent; ORDER-EDIT-001C carries no audit strings.
  10. **Reporting (M13).** Edit-retired lines (`removed_by_edit_id` set, status `voided` or `cancelled`) are excluded from Gross, Discounts and Net, and from the **Voids** bucket, which stays order-level — orders with status `voided` (`void_count`, `void_total_minor`); an order voided whole after an edit still reports its full grand total there. They are never silently dropped: a separate **Order edits** bucket per tenant scope and business day, on the order's `created_at` bucket (M12), reports `edit_count` and `edited_order_count`; `removed_minor` = Σ `line_total_minor` of retired lines with no replacement row; `replaced_out_minor` = Σ `line_total_minor` of retired lines that a row with `replaces_order_item_id` replaces; `replaced_in_minor` = Σ `line_total_minor` of those live replacement or continuation rows; `added_minor` = Σ `line_total_minor` of live rows with `edit_id` set and no `replaces_order_item_id` (added lines and +N delta rows); `net_change_minor` = `replaced_in_minor` + `added_minor` − `removed_minor` − `replaced_out_minor`. Each figure is also broken down by `reason_code` and by employee. The gross retired value (`removed_minor` + `replaced_out_minor`) is always shown, so the gross amounts stay visible as MONEY §12.2 requires; net of replacements is a derived column and never replaces it. The reader is the new read-only `owner_order_edits` ([API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.47, ORDER-EDIT-001G), never a void bucket. `owner_order_history` and `owner_active_orders` item counts exclude lines with `removed_by_edit_id` (counts for unedited and voided orders unchanged). Worked example (tax off): Burger 4000 (+Tomato, +Cucumber, both free), Fries 1500, Cola 800 → 6300; the edit takes the tomato off the burger, removes the fries and adds a Lemonade 900 → 5700; Order edits: `removed_minor` 1500, `replaced_out_minor` 4000 / `replaced_in_minor` 4000, `added_minor` 900, `net_change_minor` −600, matching the audit 6300 → 5700; Voids unchanged.
  11. **Concurrency and idempotency.** Per-line liveness plus mandatory expected totals, **no `expected_revision`** (KDS status bumps raise revision constantly). Every referenced id must be a live line of this order — foreign, retired and missing ids all return the same `line_changed` `{stale_ids}`. `app.edit_order` locks `orders` FOR UPDATE first (scoped; otherwise the 42501 anti-oracle), then the order's rounds, the referenced items and the menu items, each in id order, as every order writer locks the order row first. Business replay: an `order_edits` row for (org, device, operation id) on the same order returns the stored envelope with `idempotency_replay: true` (the same dispatch included, **D-044**); on another order it RAISES 40001. On `line_changed` or `totals_mismatch` the POS rebases automatically (re-fetch, re-apply the cashier's intents to the fresh lines, show what changed, resend under a new operation id); a transport failure retries the same identity, which is journaled durably before dispatch.
  12. **No standalone item RPC.** [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.2 `update_order_item` and §4.6 `void_item` are realized for open, unpaid orders by §4.45 (`order.edit`); no standalone item RPC is added.
  13. **Read surface.** `app.pos_order_detail` adds per item `unit_status`, `legacy` (M1a) and `edit_id`; per modifier `modifier_option_id` and the display-order snapshots; per order `dispatch_mode`, `kitchen_channel`, both switches, `edit_count`, `has_active_round` (any `order_service_rounds` row in `submitted`..`ready`) and `edits[…]` (re-emitted by POS-ORDER-DETAIL-IDS-001, then ORDER-EDIT-001B). `app.pos_order_snapshots` adds `edit_count`, `kitchen_edit_ack_pending` and `has_active_round`; the Dashboard active-order and history reads add `has_active_round` (ORDER-EDIT-001G). `app.pin_session_capabilities` adds an advisory `void_order` boolean and `branch_features` `{order_edit_enabled, order_edit_finished_food_manager_only}` (additive jsonb; no signature change; no `set_staff_capabilities` arity change). The `order_edits` pull entity is **D-044** point 2.
- **Alternatives considered:**
  - *Delete-and-recreate under the same order number* (the owner's first idea) — rejected for the reasons in Context: a new row cannot carry the same code without counterfeiting it, the old order can only be retired by a void, the kitchen double-cooks or misses the removal, and it is the cashier fraud pattern D-013 / D-020 / MONEY §13 close.
  - *New item or order states such as `edited` or `removed`* — rejected: the **D-018** enumerations are fixed; the provenance columns carry the edit.
  - *Standalone `update_order_item` / `void_item` RPCs* — rejected (point 12): §4.45 realizes them for open, unpaid orders.
  - *`expected_revision` optimistic concurrency* — rejected (point 11): KDS status bumps raise revision constantly.
  - *Clamp an order discount that exceeds the new subtotal* — rejected (owner default, 2026-10-08): it is refused and the POS offers "Lower discount" (M6).
  - *On printer-only branches with the finished-food switch ON, treat every sent line as finished food* (so a cashier gets `finished_food_needs_manager`) — rejected (owner default, 2026-10-08): the switch has no effect on the paper channel (point 8).
- **Consequences:**
  - (+) The same order and number survive an edit; every edit is attributable per line, reported per cashier and per reason, and never shown as a cancellation; no state is added.
  - (+) Reports reconcile: subtotal = Σ live line totals after every kind, and the gross retired value stays visible in the Order edits bucket.
  - (−) **RISK R-002:** concurrent tills — serialised by the order lock, per-line liveness and mandatory expected totals; every refusal is decided before the first write and leaves the order unchanged, and on `line_changed` / `totals_mismatch` the POS rebases.
  - (−) **RISK R-003:** a new table, columns and pull entity on the tenant path — FORCE RLS on `order_edits`, the 42501 anti-oracle, and test T-019 ([SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §14) with at least two organizations.
  - (−) **RISK R-007:** edits are online only, with the PIN session and membership re-validated at apply time; the cost is that an order still queued offline cannot be edited (**OPEN QUESTION Q-045**).
  - (−) **RISK R-008:** the whole-order tax recompute on tax-enabled branches (**OPEN QUESTION Q-043**); a percentage discount stays a shekel amount (**OPEN QUESTION Q-042**); `invalid_discount` / `discount_exceeds_order_total` adds friction.
  - (−) Threat **TH-7** (post-send edit-down, sweethearting; [SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §13): detected, not prevented, wherever cashiers may remove finished food — on KDS branches while the finished-food switch is OFF, and on every printer-only branch whatever the switch says.
  - (−) `app.edit_order` is the first live writer of item `cancelled` and of voided lines on live orders: a grep-based reader audit is part of ORDER-EDIT-001A's review. The `app.sync_push` re-emit (the hottest function) and the unit-closure / completion changes are mitigated by a byte-for-byte diff against the live body, the source-pin suites, the full sync pgTAP run and an exhaustive pgTAP matrix.
  - (−) Delivery load: about eleven PRs across the database, sync, three packages, two apps and the Dashboard.
- **Related:** [ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN](ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN.md) (design record), [STATE_MACHINES](STATE_MACHINES.md) §1, §1.1, §1.2, §1.3, §2.1–§2.2, §11; [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.2, §4.5, §4.6, §4.14, §4.15, §4.30, §4.30b, §4.30c, §4.32b, §4.32d, §4.33, §4.35, §4.38, §4.45, §4.46, §4.47; [MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC](MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC.md) §1, §4.4, §4.5, §6, §9.2, §12.2, §13, §19; [SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §5, §13 TH-7, §14 T-019; [DOMAIN_MODEL](DOMAIN_MODEL.md) §2.3, §6.1, §6.2, §6.4; [OFFLINE_SYNC_SPEC](OFFLINE_SYNC_SPEC.md) §1, §3, §10, §10.1, §14.1, §18; [TESTING_STRATEGY](TESTING_STRATEGY.md) §2 (T-019), §3; [IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST](IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md) (ORDER-EDIT-001 and its proposed slices); D-007, D-008, D-011, D-012, D-013, D-018, D-020, D-021, D-022, D-023, D-024, D-037, D-044; [OPEN_QUESTIONS](OPEN_QUESTIONS.md) Q-042..Q-046; **RISK R-002**, **RISK R-003**, **RISK R-007**, **RISK R-008**.

## D-044 — ORDER-EDIT-001: the kitchen is told precisely — a KDS change overlay confirmed per change (`order.edit_ack` → `app.kitchen_ack_order_edit`), one change slip on printer-only branches, the `order_edit` dispatch

- **Status:** **Owner-approved** by the human owner, Saleh, on 2026-10-08 under Work ID **ORDER-EDIT-001**: owner decisions 3 and 4, recorded in [ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN](ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN.md) §4 (PR #302). Processed, written on `main` and taking effect exactly as **D-043** (architecture-change procedure, [AGENT_WORKFLOW.md](AGENT_WORKFLOW.md) §9; effective when the ORDER-EDIT-001 register transcription is merged to `main`); documentation only. Implemented in the same design §10 slices as **D-043** — chiefly KITCHEN-DISPATCH-HARDEN-001 (precursor hardening), ORDER-EDIT-001A (server), ORDER-EDIT-001B (read and sync surface), ORDER-EDIT-001C (shared packages), ORDER-EDIT-001D (KDS app) and ORDER-EDIT-001F (POS paper), released under ORDER-EDIT-001R — each under its own Work ID, branch and PR with independent read-only review and owner approval before merge.
- **Context:** Today the kitchen learns of a change only through add-items rounds (PSC-001C) or a whole-order void (the PSC-001D red card on the KDS; the `*** VOID ***` slip on printer-only branches). The KDS has no `kitchen_tickets` tables: it builds tickets on the client from order, item, modifier and round rows pulled every **5 seconds** (no realtime receive path in production); voided lines silently disappear, a work unit whose lines are all voided vanishes, and the first-seen highlight is keyed by ticket id, so an edited ticket would never re-alert. On printer-only branches the acting POS prints directly, and the `kitchen_print_dispatches` ledger (`initial_order`, `service_round`, `void`) is drained by the POS spool only at startup, resume or context refresh (KITCHEN-MODE-001C2B); the ledger's key function maps any unknown dispatch type to `void:<order_id>`, and the payload builders do not filter item status. A full re-send is cooked twice or misses the removal, and paper cannot be recalled (**D-043**). Owner decision 3: the kitchen confirms **every change to a ticket on screen**, including a ticket not yet acknowledged, because cooks often start before tapping Acknowledge; restaurants without a kitchen screen run `kitchen_workflow_mode = 'printer_only'`, where there is no confirmation step and the change slip prints at once. Owner decision 4: the printer-only change slip carries the changes **plus** the full "ORDER NOW" list, footer "Replaces earlier tickets for #code".
- **Decision:** **DECISION D-044** —
  1. **Kitchen channel.** An edit's channel is **paper** iff `branches.kitchen_workflow_mode = 'printer_only'` OR `orders.dispatch_mode = 'direct_print'`, otherwise **kds**; it is stored on `order_edits.kitchen_channel`. A direct-print order on a branch that has since switched to KDS is refused (`kitchen_mode_changed`); a KDS order on a branch now printer-only goes to paper (mode switches while orders are open: **OPEN QUESTION Q-046**). Direct-print orders stay off the KDS.
  2. **KDS data and rendering.** `order_edits` is a new `app.sync_pull` / `app.sync_pull_changes` entity with the KDS containment (kitchen staff on printer-only branches get nothing; the direct-print filter applies), plus the non-money provenance columns on rows the KDS already pulls; the "was" text comes from the retired row, which is still pulled. A change appears within about one 5 s poll, signalled visually (no audio in v1). Rendering is a new, separate post-pass in the KDS ticket mapper (`packages/feature_kitchen/lib/src/kds_ticket_mapper.dart`); grouping, FIFO, counts (live lines only) and the one-card-per-work-unit rule are unchanged. The affected card gets an amber header "CHANGED · Change N · time · reason"; live lines are badged NEW / CHANGED (was: …) / "+N"; removed lines are shown struck through, "REMOVED". A unit emptied by the edit shows a standalone amber card "All items of this ticket removed" in its former column (mirroring the PSC-001D card); a ticket opened by an edit is labelled "Change N · Round M", with lines tagged NEW or REMAKE "instead of: …". Stage labels are per work unit ("In kitchen"), never per dish. **Alert:** a finite visual pulse keyed by (work unit, edit number), so every edit re-alerts.
  3. **Confirmation rule (owner decision 3).** On the KDS channel an edit requires a kitchen confirmation (`order_edits.kitchen_ack_required`) when it wrote to, or removed from, a work unit that was `submitted`, `accepted`, `preparing` or `ready` at commit — including a ticket the cook has not yet acknowledged. No confirmation when the edit only opened a new ticket or only touched served food, and never on paper. While a unit has a pending change its advance button (and Acknowledge on a new ticket) is replaced by **"Got it"**. `app.edit_order` returns `kitchen_ack_required`, so the POS says "kitchen must confirm" and never claims the kitchen "hadn't started".
  4. **`order.edit_ack` → `app.kitchen_ack_order_edit`.** A new online-only direct sync op through `app.sync_push`, payload `{order_id, up_to_edit_number}`. KDS-class devices only — a POS device gets `invalid_device_type` — and roles kitchen_staff, manager, restaurant_owner, org_owner, with the role denial `app.kitchen_ack_void` returns (`permission_denied`); `orders` FOR UPDATE scoped to the session (42501 anti-oracle); `invalid_edit_number` when N > `edit_count`. It stamps the write-once acknowledgement triple on every pending required edit ≤ N, so one tap covers every change up to N, clears on every KDS of the branch within one poll and never hides a newer edit; idempotent (nothing pending → ok with `acknowledged_count` 0); **no write to `orders` and no revision bump**, and it never blocks payment or status progress on the server. On a `voided` order it returns `order_voided` and stamps nothing; every other status, `served` and `completed` included, is accepted. Audited `order.edit_acknowledged`; every RETURNed refusal `order.edit_ack_denied`. Contract: [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.46.
  5. **Admission and the whole-order void.** An order with a pending confirmation stays on the board for its change cards even if it was paid and completed meanwhile, so a removal is never hidden — but never when **voided**: a whole-order void supersedes every pending edit confirmation, and only `kitchen_ack_void` clears it. Readers treat a pending required edit on a voided order as not pending (`pos_order_snapshots.kitchen_edit_ack_pending` false; no change card and no "Got it"). `app.void_order` (live `20260725090000`) is re-emitted with **one** surgical delta, everything else byte-for-byte unchanged: `kitchen_ack_required` and the matching `order.voided` audit scalar become TRUE when the order status is `submitted`..`ready`, **or** any live, non-deleted service round of the order is `submitted`..`ready`, **or** an `order_edits` row of the order has `kitchen_ack_required` true and no acknowledgement — so a parent that an edit (D-043 point 6), or today's add-items after served, left at `served` while kitchen work is still live never loses its live rounds without a red card. The red card includes round items and excludes lines with `removed_by_edit_id`; once it is acknowledged, or if no acknowledgement was required, the order leaves the board. The printer-only VOID dispatch predicate is unchanged, because an edit always leaves an `order_edit` dispatch.
  6. **Kitchen printers on KDS branches.** Print-on-Acknowledge prints the ticket **re-derived after the post-push pull** (today it prints the ticket captured at tap time), so an edited, unacknowledged ticket prints fresh. When the device auto-prints, "Got it" prints one money-free change chit for units already printed (stage `accepted`..`ready` at edit); dishes that landed in a new round print on that round's own Acknowledge, so no dish appears on two papers.
  7. **Paper: one change slip, exactly once (owner decision 4).** No confirmation step. When the server answers `applied`, the acting POS prints **one** money-free change slip through the shared kitchen ticket builder (new `KitchenTicketDocumentKind.orderChange`, ar / he / en with the **OPEN QUESTION Q-015** raster fallback): `*** ORDER CHANGED · Change N ***`; the same #code, order type, table or customer, time, staff first name and reason; REMOVED (thermal printers cannot strike through); CHANGE "was → now" and quantity changes; ADD (new lines and "+N" increases that land in the edit's round) — paper never prints REMAKE, a modified line appears only as CHANGE; **ORDER NOW** — every live line of the order; footer "Replaces earlier tickets for #code". Guard key `<orderId>|edit:<orderEditId>`; the print result is **awaited** (today's addition print is fire-and-forget); anything short of "sent" raises a persistent "Kitchen change slip not printed · Print again" banner that reprints the same document under the same guard. A newer edit of the same order retires every older pending "Print again" banner, because its ORDER NOW list is authoritative; before any reprint the POS re-reads the order's `edit_count` and, if a newer edit exists (also one made on another till), offers that edit's slip instead.
  8. **The `order_edit` dispatch (durable backup).** In the same transaction `app.edit_order` writes a `kitchen_print_dispatches` row with `dispatch_type` `order_edit` (key `edit:<order_edit_id>`; new column `order_edit_id` with CHECK ((type = 'order_edit') = (order_edit_id is not null)); money-free payload, 32 KB cap) through a new internal creator, leaving the pinned 10-argument `create_kitchen_dispatch` untouched; `app.edit_order` returns it as `kitchen_dispatch {id, claim_expires_at}` (paper channel only), and an idempotent replay returns the same dispatch. The row is created **already claimed by the acting POS** (`claimed_at = now()`, `claimed_by_device_id = p_device_id`, `claim_expires_at = now() + 10 minutes`; the KIOSK-PRINT-114B.1 claim-at-submit pattern), so no other till's drain can claim it while the acting POS prints. `acknowledge_kitchen_print_dispatch` accepts only the claim holder, so the acting POS reports it itself: `transport_accepted` after an awaited direct print that reports sent (also after the lease has expired, as long as no other device has claimed it); `failed_retryable` on any other outcome, with the "Print again" banner; `transport_accepted` after a successful "Print again". Only a dispatch whose lease lapses while still unresolved is printed by another till's drain; a later "Print again" that then gets `not_claim_owner` is a possible duplicate slip — the documented residual. The POS spool imports `order_edit` dispatches, consults the local print claim (so the acting till prints no second slip if a re-drive serves the dispatch back to it) and then sweeps superseded local jobs. `app.pull_kitchen_print_dispatches` is unchanged: `order_edit` takes the existing ELSE rank 2, safe because edits are single-op online transactions and a VOID always supersedes an edit. Audited `kitchen.dispatch_created` with `dispatch_type` `order_edit`.
  9. **Supersession shape** (replaces the CORRECTION-001 "chain length exactly 1" rule). An `order_edit` dispatch supersedes the order's unresolved earlier `initial_order` / `service_round` / `order_edit` dispatches, so stale paper never prints after a change slip; a later VOID supersedes it; a `void` row can never be superseded. `superseded_by_dispatch_id` is **write-once** (NULL → value only; any later change RAISES 23514); the target's type must be `void` or `order_edit`, in the same org and order (existing composite FK, no-self CHECK). The "target is itself unsuperseded" check runs only when the pointer is being set (`TG_OP = 'INSERT'` or `OLD.superseded_by_dispatch_id IS NULL`), so later status, claim or report updates on an already-superseded row are not re-checked. A superseder points only the order's still-unsuperseded, uncompleted rows at the dispatch it just inserted (already-superseded rows are not re-pointed), so chains such as initial → edit1 → edit2 → void are allowed; cycles stay impossible, because every edge points from an older row to a strictly newer dispatch of the same order, created under the order lock, and the pointer is write-once with an unsuperseded target at the moment it is set. Readers only test `superseded_by_dispatch_id IS NOT NULL` and never walk a chain; the guard's COMMENT is updated to match.
  10. **Precursor hardening (no behaviour change today; KITCHEN-DISPATCH-HARDEN-001).** `app.create_kitchen_dispatch` gets an explicit `void` arm and RAISES on an unknown type (today its ELSE maps any type to `void:<order_id>`); `kitchen_dispatch_payload_initial` and `_round` exclude voided and cancelled lines.
  11. **Latency and rollout.** The printer-only spool keeps the KITCHEN-MODE-001C2B startup / resume / context-refresh cadence (`apps/pos/lib/src/spool/pos_kitchen_spool_runtime.dart`); the awaited direct print plus "Print again" is the v1 safety net. A periodic spool drain changes that cadence and is out of scope: KITCHEN-SPOOL-DRAIN-002 records its own decision under the next free D-ID when approved. **Rollout rule:** deploy the migrations first (dark and backward compatible: old apps never send `order.edit` and ignore the new keys and entity), update **every KDS, then every POS** of the branch, and only then turn on `order_edit_enabled` — an old KDS would silently drop removed lines and an old POS spool would reject the `order_edit` dispatch, so the switch is the gate and the fast rollback (APKs cannot be downgraded, [ANDROID_FLEET_UPDATE_AND_ROLLBACK](ANDROID_FLEET_UPDATE_AND_ROLLBACK.md)).
- **Alternatives considered:**
  - *A full re-send or reprint of the ticket* — rejected: after cooking has started it is either cooked twice or the removal is missed, and printed paper cannot be recalled (**D-043** Context).
  - *No KDS confirmation* — rejected by owner decision 3.
  - *An audio alert* — **DEFERRED** to KDS-REALTIME-001 (with realtime, sub-second KDS updates).
- **Consequences:**
  - (+) The cook sees exactly what changed on the same card, confirms every on-screen change with one tap, and a removal is never hidden; the paper kitchen gets one authoritative slip, and no stale initial / round / edit paper prints after it.
  - (−) **RISK R-002:** the kitchen can still miss a change — the KDS is up to about 5 s behind with a visual-only alert, and the paper backup drains only at startup / resume / context refresh; mitigated by confirmation on every on-screen change, the per-edit re-alert, the awaited print with "Print again" and the dispatch claimed by the acting POS. A lapsed lease can yield a duplicate slip (documented residual).
  - (−) **RISK R-007:** both ops are online-only direct ops through `app.sync_push`, so the PIN session is validated at apply time; the KDS cannot acknowledge a change offline.
  - (−) Version skew: if the switch is enabled before every device is updated, an old KDS silently drops removed lines and an old POS spool rejects the `order_edit` dispatch; gated by the default-OFF branch switch and the rollout rule, which the setting explains.
  - (−) The widened `void_order` ack-required rule and the supersession guard change live kitchen behaviour; they are covered by the design §10 key tests (supersession chains, void after edit, the two-till printer-only scenario). Threat **TH-7** / test **T-019** ([SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §13 / §14): every kitchen output stays money-free.
- **Related:** [ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN](ORDER_EDIT_DESIGN.md) §7.2–§7.3, §8.4–§8.5; [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.6 (PSC-001D acknowledgement rule), §4.14, §4.15, §4.30c, §4.33, §4.35, §4.41 (dispatch mode), §4.45 (§4.45.4, §4.45.8, §4.45.9), §4.46; [STATE_MACHINES](STATE_MACHINES.md) §11; [PRINTERS_AND_HARDWARE_SPEC](PRINTERS_AND_HARDWARE_SPEC.md) §13.1; [OFFLINE_SYNC_SPEC](OFFLINE_SYNC_SPEC.md) §10, §10.1, §14.1; [DOMAIN_MODEL](DOMAIN_MODEL.md) §2.3, §6.4; [SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §13 TH-7, §14 T-019; [ANDROID_FLEET_UPDATE_AND_ROLLBACK](ANDROID_FLEET_UPDATE_AND_ROLLBACK.md); D-010, D-013, D-014, D-043; [OPEN_QUESTIONS](OPEN_QUESTIONS.md) Q-015, Q-046; **RISK R-002**, **RISK R-007**.

## D-045 — WEB-ORDERS-001: private pending customer requests behind a bounded server API
- **Status:** PROPOSED — owner authorized implementation on 2026-10-13; independent review and human security/merge approval remain required.
- **Decision:** Customer pickup submissions create `web_order_requests`, never canonical `orders`. Full tenant/restaurant/branch scope comes from the published Storefront slug. The server validates live catalog eligibility and recalculates integer-minor item/modifier prices and exclusive tax from authoritative database values. Requests expire after 15 minutes; a hash-only 256-bit customer capability permits a minimal private status projection for 24 hours.
- **Security boundary:** The new `web-order-submit` HTTP endpoint intentionally accepts anonymous requests (`verify_jwt=false`). Its server-only platform credential invokes exactly two service-only, invoker `public` wrappers over private `app` definer bodies. This is a narrow server-boundary exception to D-011, not permission for service credentials in clients or existing RPCs. PUBLIC, anon and authenticated are denied on both layers and all new tables. The anonymous database allowlist and public definer set remain exactly `storefront_menu(text)` (D-037/D-039).
- **Reliability and abuse:** Scoped identity/body/token fingerprint, transaction locks and unique constraints provide stable replay, conflict detection and serialized admission. Fixed per-branch minute/day/status budgets, pending capacity and phone caps bound accepted work without trusting client IP headers. There are no automatic write retries after an uncertain transport outcome. Received events are append-only and do not invent a staff identity.
- **Dark release:** Both `WEB_ORDERS_ENABLED=true` and a separately enabled branch channel are required. No enabled row is seeded; existing Storefront ordering/delivery flags and checkout remain OFF. No hosted apply or deployment is authorized by this implementation.
- **Future integration:** POS acceptance must reuse real device/PIN authorization, lock a pending unexpired request, revalidate its quote, and create/map exactly one canonical order atomically. Only then may existing KDS/printer dispatch occur. No inbox/accept/reject action is added here. See [WEB_ORDERS_001](WEB_ORDERS_001.md) and [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.48.

## D-046 — POS-ONLINE-INBOX-001: real POS authority and atomic acceptance of pending pickup requests
- **Status:** PROPOSED — implementation explicitly owner-authorized 2026-10-14; independent review and human merge/security approval remain required.
- **Context:** WEB-ORDERS-001 holds immutable private customer intent outside canonical orders. Only a real paired POS and live employee PIN may inspect or decide it. A transport's anonymous GoTrue user is not the staff actor.
- **Decision:** Add three narrow authenticated inbox/accept/reject INVOKER wrappers over private app DEFINER functions. Prove the device token and matching live PIN/session, employee, role, scope and acceptance shift on the server. Keep direct web-table access denied; record terminal decisions in a private append-only attributed ledger. Under a scoped request lock, revalidate current availability and the entire immutable price quote; changed quotes require a new request.
- **Atomicity:** Server-build one order.submit operation and call existing app.sync_push inside the decision transaction. Require its individual applied result and expected canonical ID. Canonical creation, request mapping, attributed decision and final expiry check share one rollback boundary, including rollback of failed sync ledger writes. Same-action retries return the existing mapping; cross-device competing decisions cannot duplicate it. Existing canonical RPCs and KDS/printer orchestration are preserved.
- **Catalog concurrency:** Existing catalog reparent/reorder writers may cause a deadlock or serialization abort. Propagate that whole-transaction failure for same-identity recovery; never retain a partial acceptance or silently create another decision. No canonical/catalog writer rewrite is included.
- **Alternatives/consequences:** Direct canonical inserts, a deferred client sync, copying only submit_order while omitting its orchestration tails, and silent repricing are rejected. The POS inbox requires connectivity and memory-only sensitive state; existing offline POS orders remain unchanged. No hosted rollout or public-ordering activation is included. Full contract and tests: [POS_ONLINE_INBOX_001](POS_ONLINE_INBOX_001.md), API §4.49.

New open questions **Q-039**, **Q-040** and **Q-041**. The range becomes **D-001..D-029, D-031..D-041** (**D-030** reserved/unratified); the next free ID is **D-042+**.

## D-047 — WEB-ORDERS-CHECKOUT-001: direct customer transport, capability-only recovery and line notes

- **Status:** Implementation scope owner-approved on 2026-10-14 under **WEB-ORDERS-CHECKOUT-001**, decisions A/B/C. One independent review and owner security/merge approval remain gates; this record does not authorize hosted rollout.
- **Decision:** Direct browser HTTPS POST only to the exact configured Supabase `web-order-submit` origin; narrow CSP/source-rule exceptions, no generic proxy or Next API route. Only slug, UUIDv4, 256-bit status capability and bounded metadata may enter tab-scoped sessionStorage for at most 24 hours. Frozen customer/cart payload remains memory-only; uncertain writes reconcile before explicit same-identity retry; reload cannot recreate the lost payload. Optional validated item notes are part of normalized identity and immutable snapshots, independent of pricing and order-level notes, and flow through the existing canonical item notes on trusted POS acceptance.
- **Preserved:** D-045 admission, price authority and private status capability; D-046 real device/PIN authority, atomic canonical mapping and dispatch. No online payment/delivery or automatic activation. Live browse-only gates remain OFF until separately authorized release.
- **Risks and verification:** R-002 ambiguous retries; R-003 capability/tenant boundaries; R-008 authoritative money. Focused HTTP/SQL/client/browser tests, explicit storage/credentials/CSP guards and one independent review. See [execution packet](WEB_ORDERS_CHECKOUT_001.md).

## DECISION D-048 — Persistent Maps Burger Pickup with exact-device web-print admission

- **Status:** Owner-approved objective and implementation scope on 2026-10-17 under **BIZBOT-MAPSBURGER-ALWAYS-ON-WEB-ORDERS-001**. This entry records the resulting implementation proposal; explicit human merge approval and verified hosted activation remain separate gates.
- **Context:** The owner requires successive ordinary website customers without pilot deadlines/contact restrictions, while preserving BLOCKED ordinary printing, 625 immutable historical holds and installed POS v63.
- **Decision:** Add default-OFF private exact-branch rollout authority and a service-only live submission RPC that delegates existing pricing, validation, quotas and idempotency. Atomically enroll only fresh live requests. Trusted acceptance serializes each allowed device and creates an exact request/decision/order/dispatch/session lease in the canonical transaction. Project v63's existing `target_only` contract; never grant ordinary authority. Intake OFF preserves status and admitted work; emergency print OFF revokes future claim/begin authority and creates a new generation on re-enable.
- **Alternatives:** Ordinary mode could authorize unknown historical spool work and is excluded. Repeated operator pilot windows fail the persistent-ordering requirement. An APK change is unnecessary for the reviewed exact-lease projection.
- **Consequences:** One unresolved acceptance/print slot per device; a competing acceptance is refused before canonical creation. Unsent expiry retires only that exact lease. An unfinished irreversible reservation requires reconciliation and never authorizes automatic retransmission. No old request is backfilled, no physical outcome is inferred, and no cold-offline imported-spool or commercial fleet policy is approved.
- **Verification:** Focused SQL/RLS, real local concurrent connections, unchanged-v63 containment/runtime tests, Edge/frontend tests and independent review. See [API §4.52](API_CONTRACT.md#452-persistent-scoped-pickup-admission-bizbot-mapsburger-always-on-web-orders-001) and [release/OFF runbook](MAPSBURGER_ALWAYS_ON_WEB_ORDERS.md).

## DECISION D-049 — Tenant-derived platform online ordering and continuous notification coverage

- **Status:** Owner-approved design direction and implementation scope on 2026-10-20 in GitHub Issue #353, Work ID **BIZBOT-PLATFORM-ONLINE-ORDERS-353**. This records the implementation proposal; independent security/RLS review, exact-head CI, explicit owner merge review and verified deployment/physical acceptance remain separate gates. It does not claim production readiness.
- **Context:** D-048's exact branch/device rollout and the HOME-only notification pilot cannot onboard arbitrary restaurants or newly paired POS without operator SQL. The owner explicitly requires reusable multi-tenant onboarding and continuous notifications, preserving employee authority and protected print history.
- **Decision:** Branch creation provisions scoped settings and notification scheduling state; storefront publication provisions its submission channel automatically. Future normal submissions use a new service-only platform RPC and enroll only new committed requests. All active authenticated paired POS in the branch receive the Inbox; the existing atomic decision selects one accepting POS and one exact initial-print lease. Existing workflow/printer configuration decides KDS versus printer-only routing. A fresh additive web-target permission never grants ordinary spool/drain/ACK permission. Every claim/begin remains exact and fenced by genuine readiness, immutable holds, current employee/device scope and print generation/containment revision. Existing requests/receipts and installed-device compatibility remain intact.
- **Notifications:** Continuous mode removes mandatory pilot timestamps, not emergency OFF or employee authentication. Global infrastructure is enabled once; branch/device authorization is dynamic. Instant best-effort wake and Cron share bounded fair database claims, uniqueness, leases, retries and FCM hints. Foreground renewal needs a current real PIN. Native token refresh uses status-only authority and cannot extend its deadline. Explicit optional pilot mode retains the strict UTC limit. Android permission, Force Stop, DND, battery/network restrictions and expired employee authority remain truthful delivery limitations.
- **Alternatives and consequences:** Broadcasting print jobs risks duplicate kitchen paper and is rejected. Granting ordinary authority could replay historical work and is rejected. Per-tenant UUID/config/deployment patches fail onboarding. A missing printer keeps the accepted canonical order without a fallback send. Merchant Pickup/pause/workflow controls stay scoped; no product-name, money, preparation, renderer, BLE or spool-schema changes. D-048 remains the historical/legacy contract; only future platform enrollments use the new path. See [platform contract and cutover gates](BIZBOT_PLATFORM_ONLINE_ORDERS_353.md).

## DECISION D-050 — Decision wake, public rejection consent and background reconciliation

- **Status:** Owner-approved implementation scope in Issue #356, **BIZBOT-POS-ORDER-DECISION-UX-SYNC-001**. Security/RLS review, exact-head CI, human merge approval and physical acceptance remain separate gates.
- **Decision:** Authenticated POS decisions use a narrow Edge boundary forwarding the real caller JWT, paired-device secret and PIN authority to the existing atomic decision contracts. Only a confirmed new committed terminal decision schedules the existing bounded best-effort dispatcher wake; Cron and database delivery leases remain recovery authority. Notification failures cannot change decision results or printing authority. Android reconciles the complete pending set independently of audio playback and rejects stale generations.
- **Public reason:** A dedicated explicit rejection RPC records new customer-public reasons in a private scoped append-only ledger in the same transaction as rejection. Existing rejection reasons stay private, including old-client writes and replays. Capability-authenticated status projection exposes only an optional rejected-only `customer_rejection_reason`, trimmed 1–240 Unicode code points without ASCII controls. Deploy the backward-compatible Storefront decoder before public reason production use; already-open old clients may require refresh.
- **UX and preservation:** A dedicated keyboard-aware confirmation form labels the reason as customer-visible and retains the draft across layout changes. Confirmation revalidates current request and employee authority. No cancellation, dismissal or ambiguous transport retry may create an extra decision. No tenant/device allowlists, intake/notification OFF transition, migration-history repair, protected print replay, spool or renderer change is introduced. API §4.54 defines the additive boundary.

### Storefront content implementation addendum — BIZBOT-STOREFRONT-HOME-CONTENT-001

- **Authority:** Owner-approved Issue #360 roadmap, 2026-10-21. Local implementation is authorized; exact-head human merge/RLS approval and hosted application/deployment remain separate. This entry is recorded before cross-stack content code.
- **Decision:** Extend the existing scoped Storefront profile with bounded optional Home headline, subline and announcement text. Reuse authenticated manager identity, restaurant/profile CAS and management audit/idempotency. Preserve the existing menu response and generic writer; a versioned companion menu read projects only published scoped content into the existing Home components. Empty content remains absent. See the dedicated [content contract](BIZBOT_STOREFRONT_HOME_CONTENT_001.md).
- **Preservation and concurrency:** No order, payment, notification, print, pairing or ETA authority changes. Payment-owned D-051/D-052 and their active documentation are untouched; no competing global decision number is allocated by this addendum. Decoder/backend deployment order is an explicit release gate.

## DECISION D-051 — BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001: card-present POS payments through a Z-Credit PIN pad as a flag-gated, single-tenant, TEST-mode-first pilot

- **Status:** Owner-approved objective and implementation scope on 2026-10-21 under **BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001** (command-room override of 2026-10-21: proceed from the official vendor materials already received plus test-driven discovery; further vendor correspondence is not a prerequisite for software implementation). This entry records the implementation proposal; independent review, human merge approval, hosted secret setup, migration, deployment, the owner-approved test-card run and any production enablement remain separate gates. It claims no production readiness and weakens no financial or security control. Provisional on **Q-047** (a hard owner gate on every real provider action, point 7, amended 2026-10-23), **Q-050** *(Q-053, Q-054 and Q-056 were resolved by **D-053** on 2026-10-21; amended by BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001B; Q-056 is also recorded as point 9 by the BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001A correction round 6)*. *(Status clarified 2026-10-22, BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001B round 4: **PROPOSED**, like **D-053**..**D-056**, until the independent Codex review and the human merge approval.)*
- **Context:** [MVP_SCOPE](MVP_SCOPE.md) §3 defers online/card/gateway payments to M4 and §5 forbids scaffolding a deferred capability without a decision. RF-117 already ships `card`/`bit`/`external` as *externally recorded* tenders ("RestoFlow processes no charge", [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.7). The pilot tenant needs real card-present acceptance at the till. Z-Credit's WebService PIN PAD method is server-to-server JSON with the terminal credentials in every request body, a synchronous reply that "can take several minutes", two merchant-supplied correlation keys (a status-recovery key and an exact-match duplicate-check key), a status lookup, a release call effective only while the pad shows its swipe screen, and a void/refund call ([execution packet](BIZBOT_PAYMENTS_ZCREDIT_PILOT_001.md) §3). The POS transport bound (15 s, not per-call configurable) and the Supabase Edge limits (150 s request idle timeout; 150 s / 400 s wall clock by plan) are shorter than that reply.
- **Decision:**
  1. **Scope.** Card-present payments initiated at the POS and processed on a Z-Credit PIN pad are pulled into scope as a pilot for **one tenant (Maps Burger)** behind flags that default OFF. Online, wallet, gateway-hosted, kiosk, Storefront and web-order payments stay **DEFERRED**; Storefront/Codex files, functions and worktrees remain reserved and untouched.
  2. **Architecture — initiate-and-poll with a private provider-attempt ledger.** The POS never calls the provider. One caller-JWT Edge boundary (pattern **D-050**, [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.54) proves device, PIN and employee authority in SQL, commits an attempt row to a private `app`-schema ledger **before** the first provider request, starts the provider transaction and answers within the existing bounds; the POS polls a short status read; recovery is only by the provider's status lookup keyed by the attempt's own identifier. No single HTTP request is ever relied upon to span the terminal interaction; holding a request open or continuing it in a background task is at most an optimization. *(Amended 2026-10-21, BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001B review correction: the boundary's caller-JWT path is a read-only context proof plus the manager's audited `free_order`; every app-role-callable ledger mutation that creates, claims or changes an attempt is a service-only RPC called by the adapter after that proof and re-proven server-side at write time (exceptions: exactly the three of §4.55.2 — the scope-bound provider-fact writer, the **D-054** Resume guard and the manager's caller-authority `free_order`; amended 2026-10-22, **D-056**) — [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.55.2 / §4.55.10, **D-053** point 9 — so no authenticated client can reach the ledger directly.)* *(Amended 2026-10-23, **D-057** points 1, 3 and 6: the claim guard also runs the identity-first provider recording gate for every `payment.create`; the Resume guard re-proves an active manager; operator-only RPCs take an attested owner approver and record the database executor.)*
  3. **Authoritative writer unchanged.** `app.record_payment` reached through `payment.create` ([API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.7, **D-036**) remains the only writer of `payments`. A `payments` row is created only after a verified provider approval, in today's non-cash row shape: `method='card'`, `status='completed'`, `amount_minor = tendered_minor = orders.grand_total_minor`, `change_minor = 0`. The Payment enumeration (**D-018**), terminality (**D-023**, **D-024**) and payment/fulfilment independence (**D-025**) are unchanged; declined, cancelled, released and unknown attempts never become payments. `method='card'` is classified correctly on the intended tender surfaces; the all-tender "Cash collected" label and the sales-summary fallback are separate defects owned by `DASHBOARD-COLLECTED-LABEL-001`, whose deployment — or the owner's recorded acceptance of the mislabel — gates LIVE enablement; the pilot's only Dashboard change is the exact [MVP_SCOPE](MVP_SCOPE.md) §5 exception (Dashboard verification tests, the `card_attempt.*` audit registry entries and safe-detail labels, and their ar / he / en titles) *(amended 2026-10-23, round 6)*. *(Amended 2026-10-21, 001B review correction: the body of `record_payment` stays unchanged; `app.sync_push` gains the claim guard of [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.55.3 — while an order has an active claim (§4.55.3), every competing tender, discount, sent-order edit and void is refused server-side with `payment_in_progress` and only the matching approved identity records, once; a hand-recorded `card` tender on a LIVE terminal-enabled till is refused `card_terminal_required` — **D-053** points 2 and 7.)* *(Amended 2026-10-23, **D-057** points 1 and 4: a provider identity records only as its own admissible LIVE leg under the identity-first gate — a TEST identity never — and the hand-recorded `card` refusal holds while the till's LIVE mapping is `configured`, `enabled` or `draining`.)*
  4. **TEST mode first.** The first physical test runs in TEST mode only: Maps Burger authorization scope (real paired device, PIN session and membership proof), an **order-less** provider attempt (amount-only, no `order_id`), never `record_payment`, no `payments`, receipt, shift, drawer, order-history, kitchen or Dashboard-revenue effect, one frozen identity, no automatic retry, UNKNOWN on a dropped connection, and poll/status recovery under the same identity — and only inside the owner-sealed non-settling TEST facility of point 7 *(amended 2026-10-23, round 6)*.
  5. **Runtime configuration, never source.** The provider base URL, environment, terminal binding, PIN-pad identifier and terminal credentials are runtime configuration: credentials as server-side secrets by NAME, the PIN-pad binding as a tenant-scoped server mapping that holds no credential, environment selection as configuration. None of them is hard-coded, guessed, committed, logged or shipped to a client (**D-011**).
  6. **Flags and gates.** An Edge master flag, default OFF (exact `true` enables); an operator revision pinned to the pilot organization/branch (pattern `app.set_mapsburger_web_orders`); a per-device default-OFF toggle; a branch feature read by the POS that hides the action when absent (fail closed). *(Amended 2026-10-21: the per-device toggle is UX only; the exact-device `payment_terminals` row and the operator revision are the authority, and no branch-level default authorizes a charge in phase 1 — **D-053** point 6.)* Code may be completed and tested with fakes before any provider call; no Z-Credit call, card use, terminal operation, hosted secret write, migration, deployment or live enablement happens without a separate owner approval after code review — and no real provider action happens before the sealed evidence of point 7.
  7. **Fail closed on mismatch, behind a hard non-settlement gate** *(amended 2026-10-23, BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001A correction round 6)*. Code and fake-provider work proceed without vendor evidence, but no real provider action — no provider commit, status or release call, no test-card use, no terminal payment action and no hosted provider-secret setup — happens until the owner has sealed, outside Git, an evidence package that identifies the exact non-settling TEST facility: the exact test base URL and environment; the test credential record with its mode and scope; the test terminal / PIN-pad binding; the test card's status; written or provider-portal evidence that this facility cannot settle or move real money; and the exact mapping of every supplied key to its purpose and environment (**Q-047**). The package names records by opaque references only and never puts a credential, card number or identifier in the repository. A missing, incomplete or failed package is a hard owner gate — never replaced by test-driven discovery or by making a first charge to see whether it settles. Inside the sealed facility, if the device does not prompt, the provider rejects the configured credentials or environment, or a reply differs from the received contract, the attempt ends UNKNOWN or refused with a sanitized typed outcome; nothing is retried automatically and nothing is recorded; the run stops and the sanitized mismatch is reported for a targeted vendor escalation.
  8. **Data minimisation.** PAN, CVV, test-card numbers, PINs, serials, MAC/IMEI values, provider passwords and keys, terminal credentials and unrestricted provider payloads are never persisted, logged, printed, reproduced in reports or committed; **D-052** fixes the per-field rule.
  9. **Pilot till platform — the Android POS only (resolves Q-056; added 2026-10-23, BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001A correction round 6).** Phase 1 is normatively the Android POS APK on the designated pilot device of the pilot tenant; the hosted web POS, browser tabs, kiosk and any second till are out of scope and never start a provider attempt; the provider fields ride as a sibling key of the POS attempt journal and the journal schema version is unchanged (**D-052** point 1).
- **Alternatives considered:**
  - *A synchronous proxy inside one Edge request* — rejected: the 15 s POS bound, the 150 s request idle timeout and the wall clock are shorter than the vendor's reply; an approval could be lost in flight.
  - *Provider lifecycle inside `public.payments` (`pending → tendered → completed`)* — canon-compatible (the states and the CHECK exist) but rejected for the pilot: it changes the `record_payment` contract and touches every reader of `payments`, the one-completed-payment index and the settlement predicate.
  - *An `is_test` flag on `payments`* — rejected: a test row could settle or block a real order; about 20 RPCs, 5 views and 79 pgTAP files would change.
  - *A long-running worker outside Supabase* — rejected: a second trust boundary holding provider credentials, with no evidence it buys anything the ledger does not.
  - *Direct POS → provider calls* — rejected by **D-011** (credentials travel in the request body).
- **Consequences:** (+) revenue, shift totals, receipts, settlement and Dashboard totals are isolated from test activity by construction; (+) no state-machine change and no Dashboard analytics or report change for the agreed row shape (the only Dashboard change is the [MVP_SCOPE](MVP_SCOPE.md) §5 exception, point 3); (+) every provider outcome is durable and recoverable under one identity (**D-022**). (−) a new ledger, RPCs, Edge function and shared transport capability (tickets 001B–001F in [IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST](IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md); 001G / 001H were approved as Work IDs by the owner on 2026-10-21, together with `BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-HYGIENE-001` and `DASHBOARD-COLLECTED-LABEL-001` — **D-053** point 10); (−) order history and the Activity log are protected only by the order-less TEST-mode rule and the TEST-mode audit rule (**D-053** point 4, formerly **Q-055**); (−) **RISK R-002** (ambiguous retries), **R-003** (tenant and credential boundaries), **R-007** (device or PIN revocation mid-attempt), **R-008** (money at a decimal boundary); (−) every real provider action waits for the owner's sealed evidence of the non-settling TEST facility (point 7). Open points: **Q-047..Q-050** and **Q-052** (Q-051 and Q-053..Q-057 were resolved by **D-053** on 2026-10-21).
- **Related:** **D-052**; [BIZBOT_PAYMENTS_ZCREDIT_PILOT_001](BIZBOT_PAYMENTS_ZCREDIT_PILOT_001.md); [MVP_SCOPE](MVP_SCOPE.md) §2.5 / §3 / §5; [STATE_MACHINES](STATE_MACHINES.md) §5; [MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC](MONEY_AND_TAX_SPEC.md) §1 / §2 / §8 / §11 / §12.3 / §17; [SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL](SECURITY_AND_THREAT_MODEL.md) §12 / TH-8 / T-023; [API_CONTRACT](API_CONTRACT.md) §4.7, §4.35, §4.54, §4.55 (the contract changes land under BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001B). **D-056** (status and point 2 amended 2026-10-22, round 4). **D-057** (points 2 and 3 amended 2026-10-23, round 5).

## DECISION D-052 — Provider-attempt lifecycle, identity, money and data-minimisation rules for card-present payments

- **Status:** Owner-approved scope on 2026-10-21 under **BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001** (the same override as **D-051**); records the implementation proposal; independent review and human merge approval remain gates. Defaults the owner may amend before merge are marked **ASSUMPTION**. Provisional on **Q-048**, **Q-049**, **Q-052** *(Q-051 and Q-055 were resolved by **D-053** on 2026-10-21; amended by BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001B)*. *(Status clarified 2026-10-22, BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001B round 4: **PROPOSED**, like **D-053**..**D-056**, until the independent Codex review and the human merge approval.)*
- **Context:** The provider's observable states (commit reply, status-lookup codes, duplicate-check refusals, release, void/refund) must map onto RestoFlow's frozen rules without new Payment states, without a hidden refund path and without floating-point money, while the vendor's reply may outlive every request bound.
- **Decision:**
  1. **Attempt states are not Payment states.** A provider attempt is a private ledger record with states `sent → approved | declined | unknown | released`, then `approved → recorded` (the `payments` row exists through `payment.create`) or `approved → approved_unrecorded` (approval verified, recording refused or impossible). `unknown` is a normal, durable state resolved only by the provider status lookup. `released` is provisional: it is entered only when the provider confirms the release and it resolves by a later status lookup (`released → approved | declined`), so an approval discovered after a release is still written to the ledger as `approved` and recorded only while its claim is still active (amended 2026-10-22, **D-056** point 9). These states live in the ledger and in the POS attempt journal, never in `payments.status`; **D-018** is unchanged.
  2. **One identity; both provider keys derived from its full tuple.** The internal source identity of an attempt is the full tuple `organization_id` + `device_id` + `local_operation_id` — the frozen POS attempt identity (**D-022**) within its tenant — and never `local_operation_id` alone. Two domain-separated, versioned digests of that tuple — one for the status-recovery key, one for the duplicate-check key, each over its own domain label and version — are computed server-side when the attempt is claimed and persisted exactly, in full; the outbound encoding, length and character set of each merchant-supplied key come only from sealed **Q-049** evidence carried by a versioned formatter configuration, and without that evidence or configuration `start` answers `provider_unavailable` with zero provider calls; the exact outbound strings are stored as sent. A collision — of a digest or of an outbound projection — can only over-serialize or refuse safely (a key already held by another attempt of the organization is refused before any provider request) and never lets two attempts share a provider transaction *(amended 2026-10-23, BIZBOT-PAYMENTS-ZCREDIT-PILOT-001A correction round 6; the 001B contract fixes the digest and formatter details)*. An exact-repeat refusal from the provider means "already submitted": the attempt is looked up, never re-charged. A new attempt may be minted only after a conclusive (final-state) status-lookup result or, for a lookup that stays UNKNOWN, after the manager's confirmation-bound `free_order` of the **Q-048** interim (an explicit provider / back-office confirmation that no approved charge exists — poll-cap exhaustion alone never frees an order, **D-053** point 1; amended 2026-10-21) — never while an attempt is in progress; only one attempt per order may be in progress: the per-order claim is taken server-side before the first provider request, so a second device or a duplicate request is refused before any charge.
